LEFT JOIN [ScrapMolinos] AS [s] ON [h].[Id_Scrap] = [s].[IdScrap]
LEFT JOIN [Lineas] AS [l] ON [p].[Id_Linea] = [l].[Id_linea]
LEFT JOIN [EstadosSap] AS [e] ON [h].[IdEstatusSAP] = [e].[Id]
LEFT JOIN [EnvioScraps] AS [e0] ON [s].[IdEnvioScrap] = [e0].[IdEnvioScrap]
WHERE ([p].[Id] IS NOT NULL) AND [p].[Planta] = @__planta_0 AND ((([p].[Id] IS NOT NULL) AND [p].[FechaPesaje] >= @__Parse_1 AND [p].[FechaPesaje] <= @__Parse_2) OR (([s].[IdScrap] IS NOT NULL) AND [s].[FechaScrap] >= @__Parse_1 AND [s].[FechaScrap] <= @__Parse_2))
ORDER BY [p].[FechaPesaje]
